16:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
17:T450,The analysis starts with a separable strongly convex quadratic and an affine-variance noise model, where the gradient-noise variance has an additive term σ0² and a multiplicative term σ1²||∇f||². When σ0=0, the noise is a scale family and vanishes at the optimum. For SGD in one coordinate, the second moment follows an exactly multiplicative recursion with contraction r(η)=(1-ηλ)²+η²σ1²λ². The optimal fixed learning rate is η*=1/(λ(1+σ1²)), giving contraction σ1²/(1+σ1²), and the best WSD cooldown fraction is zero. For signSGD and normalized methods under the same multiplicative noise, the probability of stepping in the correct direction is independent of the iterate scale. The dynamics reduce to an analyzable lattice walk, producing a final loss floor of Θ(η²). Cooldown helps because it reduces the step granularity. With any additive noise component, σ0>0, every method has an η-dependent floor. The paper then checks robustness under local (L0,L1)-smoothness, multidimensional normalized SGD, momentum, and heavy-tailed noise without relying on finite moments.18:T51d,Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention extends KDA by inserting a block-rotational control operator before the delta correction. In real coordinates, the control is a direct sum of aligned 2×2 rotations combined with positive decay. Under the identification R^{2m} with C^m, this becomes a complex diagonal phase-decay matrix Λ_t. Each transition keeps the form A_t = Λ_t - u_t w_t^*, a phase-decay operator plus a rank-one delta correction. KDA is recovered by setting the phase to zero. The important design choice is to avoid dense control matrices; a dense phase operator would turn the chunk product into dense d×d multiplication and remove the computational advantage. The main technical result is a constructive left-to-right chunk-WY theorem for products P_t = A_t A_{t-1} ... A_1. The factors Γ_t, Y_t, M_t, and W_t are updated explicitly, and rank growth is confined to a fixed chunk. The paper also gives an exact affine chunk transfer, including an additive write summary, plus complex-to-real implementation details, formal cost bounds, a stability lemma, and a rank-growth discipline. On the expressivity side, SFDA gives compact exact constructions for cyclic counters, dihedral phase-orientation memory, register/reset memories, bounded stacks, and finite-state systems after one-hot lifting.19:T4ce,For exact unlearning, decremental deletion is compared with retraining from scratch without the removed point under the same box parameter C. On Gaussian streams, MIMIC-IV records, and learned model keys, the method recovers identical support-vector partitions in 98%–100% of trials whenever the optimum is unique. The median decision-function deviation from refit is about 1e-9, and about 1e-13 on learned keys. The worst reported deviation, 1.1e-2, occurs in ill-conditioned near-duplicate streams and remains below coefficient decay. For certified selection, rare-item recall under skewed redundancy is 0.86 at a matched token budget, compared with 0.32 for an H2O-style heavy-hitter baseline even when that baseline is given oracle access to evaluation queries. An adaptive support set keeps 0.95 recall at 40 distinct items, while a matched-state DeltaNet recurrence falls to 0.06. The paper also demonstrates surgical fact forgetting, knowledge editing, and patient deletion on MIMIC-IV streams, reporting zero leakage and 0.80 vs. 0.05 retention of critical hours. The trainable batched path reaches 9,125 tokens/s, but is 6.1x slower than an unfused PyTorch CPU softmax reference and 35.8x slower than the MPS reference.1a:T705,The core idea is hierarchical vector-quantized attention. In standard VQ-attention, keys are clustered into M codewords, and attention is computed over those representatives instead of all N keys. AVQ starts with a coarse set of parent codewords, computes attention at that level, and then refines only the most important parent regions using pre-learned child codewords. Importance is determined dynamically from the forward pass itself: codewords that receive more attention mass are refined, while the rest remain coarse.

There are three technically important pieces. First, the importance signal is endogenous. The method does not need an auxiliary predictor; it uses attention mass accumulated during the forward pass. Second, the hierarchical codebook is constrained so that each parent equals the mean of its children. This is not a cosmetic choice. It allows the parent contribution to be recovered from child logits, which avoids revisiting parent codewords during refinement. Third, the method is implemented inside a FlashAttention-style tiled computation. Since FlashAttention never materializes the full attention matrix, both importance estimation and refinement must happen at tile level and effectively in registers. The paper proposes a two-stage incremental procedure: compute parent-level attention first, then refine the top-P parents with child blocks while updating the online softmax state and correcting contributions using the parent-child constraint.

The claimed complexity remains O(MN), matching standard VQ-attention, but with adaptive allocation of representational capacity. The implementation also includes custom Triton kernels to fuse operations and reduce memory traffic. That systems component is important because theoretical savings alone are rarely enough.1b:T589,The experiments cover ImageNet-1k classification, ADE20K semantic segmentation, and high-resolution Stable Diffusion generation. The main empirical pattern is consistent: AVQ-Attention gives a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than fixed-codebook VQ-attention under similar compute budgets, and it is competitive with several existing efficient-attention baselines. The paper also highlights a practical deployment angle: both VQ and AVQ attention can be inserted post hoc into pretrained transformers and then fine-tuned for a small number of epochs, in a way analogous to quantization-aware compression workflows.

From a foundation-model perspective, the most useful experimental points are not the absolute task scores but the three validation axes. First, adaptive refinement improves over flat quantization. Second, the custom kernels help convert algorithmic savings into wall-clock gains. Third, the post-hoc replacement plus short fine-tuning route suggests a lower-cost way to test efficient attention on existing models.

The limitations are clear as well. The paper does not evaluate decoder-only LLMs, long-context language modeling, KV-cache behavior, or pretraining throughput. So it does not yet establish that the method is effective for large language model training or inference. Stability of codebook learning and dynamic refinement in autoregressive language settings remains an open question.1c:T763,The core idea is a sample-filter-select pipeline. Instead of maintaining exact scores for all candidates, the kernel first estimates where the eventual top-k region is likely to lie, then uses that threshold range to discard hopeless candidates during the main pass.

The method has three stages. First, sampling. The paper uses a locality assumption in sparse attention: adjacent token chunks often attend to similar historical tokens, so their score distributions are similar. LiteTopK therefore reuses the previous chunk’s top-k' scores as a lightweight sample; for DSA, the paper uses k' = 3k. Second, quantization and histogramming. The estimated score range is partitioned into equal-width bins, a histogram is initialized, and the bin containing the local top-k boundary is identified as the threshold bin. Third, fused filtering. During the full candidate scan, the kernel computes scores and dynamically maintains the threshold bin. The upper bound of that bin is used to filter out candidates that are unlikely to enter the final top-k, so only a small subset needs to be written back and processed further.

Several implementation choices are notable. The kernel replaces the original score computation path with direct bin-ID computation to avoid extra latency. Histogram updates are handled by idle warps using overwrite-style writes rather than lock-based synchronization; a slightly stale or loose threshold is acceptable because it does not break correctness, only filtering aggressiveness. The whole design relies on a high-dimensional concentration effect: most similarity scores lie in a narrow range, while truly large scores are rare long-tail events. Compared with exact top-k kernels, LiteTopK does not materialize the full score matrix before selection; it injects approximate distribution estimation into the main path so that low-value candidates never reach HBM.1d:T48a,From the provided full-text excerpt, the most important empirical evidence is the bottleneck characterization and the score-distribution observation. In GLM-5.2 prefill at 1M context, the DSA kernel takes 83.7% of total runtime, which validates the optimization target. On memory, the original DSA path incurs 32 GB of runtime overhead at a moderate chunk size of 8192, directly competing with KV-cache capacity and forcing systems such as vLLM to split chunks into much smaller sub-chunks, which hurts throughput.

The paper also shows a representative score distribution from the first layer: most DSA scores lie between 25 and 35, while scores above 45 are extremely rare, with only about 10K such scores under a 1M-token context. This supports the sample-filter design. The abstract and excerpt indicate comparisons against existing GPU Indexer-TopK kernels, especially DSA, with lower HBM write-back, lower memory overhead, and lower latency. However, the provided text does not include the full benchmark tables, end-to-end throughput numbers, or ablations over k, dimensionality, and batch size, so it would be unsafe to invent exact speedup figures here.1e:T48b,The evaluation covers three video VLM families: Qwen3-VL 4B/8B with causal attention, 3D MRoPE, and DeepStack feature injection into early LLM layers; Molmo2 4B/8B/O-7B with 1D sequential RoPE, bidirectional attention over vision tokens, and causal text attention; and PerceptionLM 1B/3B with 1D sequential RoPE and fully causal attention. The benchmarks are TempCompass, with 410 videos and 1,580 multiple-choice questions, and TVBench, with 2,205 multiple-choice questions. On TempCompass, action and speed are treated as less diagnostic of temporal order, while attribute change, direction, and order are used for aspect-level analysis. A key result is that Molmo2 and Qwen3-VL both gain roughly +0.20 accuracy from full video over a single frame, but reversal-drop separates them. Molmo2 is almost unchanged under reversal, suggesting position dominance. Qwen3-VL drops sharply across temporal tasks, suggesting reliance on the visual sequence. Activation patching recovers Molmo2 and PerceptionLM when corrected content is injected at the first LM layer, while Qwen3-VL retains a small residual attributed to DeepStack’s mid-layer visual feature injection.1f:T45b,The audit includes 144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192, 40,800 on LongBench, and 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout. It uses three open 7B–9B models. RULER cells use the same 650 instances and cover retrieval, aggregation, and multi-hop axes. The central result is that query visibility changes rankings. Under query-agnostic compression on RULER, among the five backend-comparable methods, only the query-independent KeyDiff consistently beats the best-of-3 trivial baseline, winning 31 of 36 cells with an average +0.171 margin. SnapKV falls below that baseline on average in the agnostic protocol, winning 13 of 36 cells with a -0.066 margin, while its query-aware gain of +0.132 is reproduced. LongBench gives a less exclusive picture: the query-agnostic issue remains valid, but other methods can catch up or overtake. The audit also finds that eager versus SDPA attention backend changes pooled RULER accuracy by -0.221, larger than many method gaps, and that RULER-8192 can overflow gemma-2’s positional budget by up to about 30%, silently zeroing 7 of 13 subtasks even without compression.20:T747,The work has two main components. The first is a capability-driven data and training design. The authors define a three-level taxonomy: Action-Relevant State Understanding, Action-Transition Reasoning, and Sequential and Adaptive Reasoning. The first level covers object parts, attributes, states, depth, metric estimation, 3D structure, viewpoint and pose, robot-centric cues, and affordances. The second level focuses on actions, local state transitions, interaction consequences, and physical constraints. The third level moves to long-horizon planning, navigation, reflection, repair, and recovery. Importantly, this taxonomy is not only used for evaluation. It is used to guide both pre-training and post-training data pipelines and mixtures.

The second component is the model design. Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 combines the Hy3-A3B language backbone with the Hy-ViT2 vision encoder and adopts an MoE architecture to keep inference efficient. The paper emphasizes 3B activated parameters while maintaining performance close to a much larger previous-generation system. The architectural novelty is limited in isolation, but the design choice is deliberate: the model is built for embodied deployment where latency matters.

From the full text, the training recipe appears to be split into pre-training and post-training stages, both informed by the taxonomy. The paper does not fully expose all mixture ratios or data scales at a level that would make the pipeline fully reproducible, but the intent is clear: the authors are not simply concatenating existing VQA, navigation, and robotics datasets. They are trying to balance training signals according to capability coverage. That is a meaningful design decision. It suggests that embodied performance bottlenecks are treated first as a training-signal organization problem, not only as a model-capacity problem.21:T694,The most important experimental contribution is the breadth of evaluation and the efficiency-performance trade-off. The model is evaluated on 38 benchmarks spanning the three capability levels. Against recent comparable-scale systems including Qwen3.6-A3B, NVIDIA Cosmos 3-8B, and Embodied-R1.5-8B, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 ranks first on 19 benchmarks and second on 11. It also achieves the best average score across all three capability categories, outperforming the strongest competitor, Qwen3.6-A3B, by 4.4% on average. Relative to Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B, the average gain is 8.4%.

Efficiency is another key result. The model uses only 3B activated parameters, roughly one tenth of the previous A32B system, while delivering nearly comparable overall performance. For embodied systems, that is more informative than total parameter count alone.

The paper also evaluates on vision-and-language navigation tasks. On R2R-CE, it reports the best result in the RGB-only setting. On Matterport3D Object Goal Navigation, it shows strong zero-shot transfer. These results are used as evidence that the model is not only improving on static perception benchmarks but is also learning capabilities related to sequential interaction and adaptation.

That said, from a pretraining perspective, the ablation story appears incomplete in the public text. The paper reports strong aggregate gains, but it is harder to isolate how much comes from the taxonomy-driven data design versus the backbone, the MoE architecture, or post-training. More granular ablations on data mixture, capability-level removal, dense versus MoE, and pretrain/post-train balance would have made the causal picture clearer.22:T44e,For a quick pass, focus on three parts. First, read the introduction and the taxonomy figure carefully. That is the conceptual center of the paper. Second, read the Data and Taxonomy section with attention to how capability definitions are translated into data construction and mixture design. That is the most useful part for multimodal pretraining researchers. Third, inspect the main benchmark table and the navigation results, especially the relation between benchmark coverage and the three capability levels, and the comparison between 3B activated parameters and the previous larger model.

A 30-minute skim is enough to extract the main ideas: introduction, taxonomy figure, main results table, navigation section, and conclusion. If reading more deeply, keep two questions in mind. What capability gaps is the data mixture trying to close? And in this setting, is MoE only reducing compute, or is it also enabling a more useful specialization pattern for embodied reasoning? The most interesting part is not any single benchmark score, but the training organization principle behind the model.23:T957,实验设计比较干净。作者选了 Qwen 3.5 的 0.8B、2B、4B 三个尺度，在 Math、Code、Instruction Following、Multilingual、Safety 五个域上分别做 QLoRA 专家微调。LoRA rank 为 16，底座 4-bit 量化，目标模块覆盖 attention 和 MLP projection。训练使用常数学习率 2e-4、100 step warmup、AdamW、effective batch size 8，而且故意不做学习率衰减，这样模型在验证最优点之后仍会持续更新，便于观察过拟合阶段的 merge 效应。每个专家先独立确定验证损失最优步数 T*，再保存 0.25x、0.5x、0.75x、1x、1.5x、2x、3x、5x T* 的 checkpoint。

随后作者把五个域的 LoRA adapter 在同一底座上做合并，比较五类方法：Simple Averaging、Task Arithmetic、TIES、DARE+TIES、Greedy Soup。Task Arithmetic 的缩放系数 λ 在 {0.5, 1.0, 1.5} 上网格搜索；TIES 和 DARE+TIES 的 density 设为 0.5。核心发现是 merge 方法对专家训练时长的偏好呈现稳定分层：
1. Simple Averaging 在较早 checkpoint 最好，随着专家继续训练明显恶化；
2. Task Arithmetic 和 Greedy Soup 处于中间区间，通常接近验证最优或略晚；
3. TIES 与 DARE+TIES 在过拟合区间达到峰值，往往要到 1.5x、2x 甚至更晚。

机制分析主要借了两个视角。一个是 bias-variance-covariance decomposition。作者把 merged model 的泛化误差拆成平均偏差、个体方差和专家间协方差，类比 random forest 中“深树虽然单体过拟合，但因相关性下降，集成后反而更好”的现象。这里的对应关系是：过训练专家虽然单体更差，但参数更新更分散、域特化更强，给 merge 带来更高多样性；若 merge 方法有能力处理冲突参数，这种多样性可以转化为收益。另一个是 mode connectivity / loss barrier 分析。作者检查不同训练时长下专家之间以及专家到 merged model 之间的连通性和障碍高度，说明过拟合后专家更远离彼此、线性插值障碍更高，简单平均因此更容易落到坏区域；而 TIES/DARE 通过裁剪小幅更新、解决符号冲突、保留一致方向的大幅 delta，能更好地从分散专家中抽取可兼容信号。这个解释比“过拟合有时有益”更具体，关键不在过拟合本身，而在过拟合带来的多样性是否能被 merge 机制消化。24:T7f7,The setup is systematic and fairly controlled. The authors use Qwen 3.5 models at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B scales. For each scale, they fine-tune domain experts with QLoRA (rank 16) on top of a 4-bit quantized base model, targeting all attention and MLP projection modules. Training uses AdamW, constant learning rate 2e-4, 100-step warmup, and effective batch size 8. The constant LR is deliberate: it keeps optimization active beyond the validation optimum so that the experts can move into clearly overfitted regimes.

They train experts on five domains—Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, and Safety. For each expert, they identify its own validation-optimal step T* and then save checkpoints at 0.25x, 0.5x, 0.75x, 1.0x, 1.5x, 2.0x, 3.0x, and 5.0x T*. These checkpoints are then merged across all five domains using five methods: Simple Averaging, Task Arithmetic, TIES, DARE+TIES, and Greedy Soup.

The central empirical pattern is method-dependent and stable across scales. Simple Averaging peaks early and degrades sharply as experts continue training. Task Arithmetic and Greedy Soup prefer an intermediate regime, often around or slightly after T*. TIES and DARE+TIES peak later, often in clearly overfitted regions. The paper then explains this with two analytical lenses. First, a bias-variance-covariance decomposition: overtrained experts may generalize worse individually, but they become more diverse, and methods with interference-resolution mechanisms can convert that diversity into gains. Second, mode connectivity / loss barrier analysis: as experts train longer, they drift farther apart in weight space, making naive linear averaging more likely to land in poor regions, while sparsification and sign-resolution help preserve compatible directions and suppress destructive conflicts. The contribution is not just the empirical observation that “overfitting can help,” but a more precise claim: later checkpoints are useful when the merge rule can harvest diversity without inheriting all the interference.25:T63f,The study spans 3 model sizes, 5 domains, 8 training durations, and 5 merging methods. The main metric aggregates the four accuracy-based domains, while Safety is evaluated separately with refuse-to-answer rate on HarmBench. The most important result is the shape of the performance curves rather than a single headline number.

Figure 1 appears to be the key result: Simple Averaging deteriorates as expert training extends beyond T*, indicating strong sensitivity to late-stage expert divergence. TIES and DARE+TIES continue improving past T* and often reach their best merged performance in overfitted regimes. Task Arithmetic and Greedy Soup sit in between. The paper states that this qualitative pattern holds across 0.8B, 2B, and 4B models, though the exact peak location and gain size vary by scale.

The analysis sections are more informative than raw averages. One recurring observation is that after T*, individual expert validation quality drops while inter-expert diversity increases. For averaging, that diversity mostly manifests as destructive interference. For TIES/DARE-style methods, part of it becomes complementary signal once small deltas are trimmed and sign conflicts are resolved. The paper also compares merged performance against the best single expert across durations, reinforcing the point that validation-optimal experts are not necessarily merge-optimal experts. The abstract and visible text do not expose many exact numeric improvements, so the paper is best read for its robust trends, peak shifts, and mechanistic analysis rather than for one isolated gain figure.26:Tab4,技术路线分成四层，而且每一层都围绕“把几何恢复和因果有效性拆开”展开。

第一层是构造完全可控的 ground-truth superposition toy model。作者从头训练一个很小的合成模型，不用预训练权重，也不用外部数据，使得真实 feature 方向、隐藏维度、稀疏度和 superposition 几何都已知。这样做的目的不是追求 realism，而是让“恢复是否正确”以及“干预是否沿真实路径生效”都能被严格定义。

第二层是复现并修正已有 SAE 几何结论。论文多 seed 复现了 Elhage 等人的 superposition phase diagram，并指出一个重要 artifact：在高 sparsity 区域，若训练步数不够，会把 under-training 误判成 superposition 结构。作者用 sparsity-aware step budgets 解决这个问题。除此之外，论文还描述了一个此前没有被充分刻画的 diffuse sharing regime：在极端 overcompleteness 下，每个 feature 都被“相当程度地表示”，但 feature dimensionality 很低，说明表示不是少数原子清晰分工，而是更弥散地共享字典容量。

第三层是复现 TopK SAE 与 L1 SAE 的比较，并把“L1 shrinkage”从口头说法变成直接测量。作者从头复现 TopK-vs-L1 Pareto，对 activation refinement 做了直接测试，量化 L1 正则导致的 shrinkage，而不是只凭经验判断。这里的关键点是：decoder recovery 看起来相近时，encoder 激活行为可能已经明显不同，这正是后面因果分歧的来源之一。

第四层是提出一套 causal validation battery，并把 inertness 细分成可操作的类型。作者对每个 correlationally recovered feature 做两类干预：ablation 和 sign-correct steering，并把影响一路传播到模型真实输出层，而不是停留在 SAE reconstruction 空间。基于这些干预，论文区分了两条轴。按成因分，有 structural inertness 和 competitive inertness。前者来自 antipodal-pair superposition geometry 与 positive-pass encoder 的结构性错配，即使 SAE 训练更好也未必消失；后者来自 TopK 竞争机制下退化字典的竞争病理，并且和数值边界不稳定性耦合。按方向分，有 read-inertness 和 write-inertness：前者表示 ablation 监测不到，后者表示 steering 无法写入有效影响。论文指出 antipodal pairs 可以把这两者完全解耦。

此外，作者还把这套审计流程封装成 sae-causal-audit 工具，接口只要求对象能 encode、decode 并暴露 dictionary，因此理论上可审计任意 SAE 实现，包括 sae_lens.SAE。工具设计里还专门处理了 sign-handling bug 这一类常见错误，使其在接口层面不易发生。27:T9d8,实验部分信息量很高，且不少结论都带具体数字。

第一，superposition phase diagram 的复现不是单次跑通，而是多 seed 统计。作者发现高 sparsity 区域存在收敛伪象：训练不够时会看起来像某种 superposition 几何，实际只是没收敛。这个问题通过 sparsity-aware step budget 解决。论文还报告了 diffuse sharing regime 的量化特征：在极端 overcompleteness 下，每个 feature 的表示强度大致为 ||W_i||^2 ≈ 1.40，而 feature dimensionality 约为 D_i ≈ 0.08，说明 feature 普遍被表示，但每个 feature 占用的“维度份额”很小。

第二，TopK-vs-L1 复现部分，作者不仅比较 Pareto，还直接测 activation refinement，验证 L1 shrinkage 的存在。虽然当前给出的正文片段没有完整表格数字，但结论很明确：仅看 decoder 对齐会高估 L1 SAE 的恢复质量，因为 encoder 激活被 shrinkage 扭曲后，因果效应会进一步偏离。

第三，也是最关键的，因果验证结果显示 correlational recovery 和 causal efficacy 可以明显分离。在一个 degraded SAE 上，22 个几何匹配到的 feature 里有 17 个是 causally inert；在一个 well-trained SAE 上，同样是 22 个匹配里只有 2 个 inert。更强的一点是，inert match 的 cosine 可以高到 0.9998，说明“几何几乎完美”并不能保证“因果上有效”。这基本击穿了只用 cosine recovery 做结论的做法。

第四，真实模型小规模审计。作者拿一个已发布的 production SAE，gpt2-small-res-jb，对 83 个手工编写、跨多个语义域的 concept 做审计，恢复出 7 个 correlationally matched feature，其中 1 个，也就是 14%，是 causally inert。样本不大，但方向上和 toy setting 一致。论文还发现一个意外现象：少数 decoder atoms 会反复成为几十个语义上互不相关 concept 的最近邻匹配。作者用三组独立构造、规模和多样性逐步增加的 concept batch 复现了这个 atom collision 现象，并用受控负例排除了 prompt template artifact 的解释。

第五，复现性实验。作者在 CI 调试中说明了为什么浮点训练跨平台做不到 byte-exact reproducibility，并提出两层保证：固定环境内做 SHA-256 级别的字节一致；跨环境则要求 semantic equality，数值容差 rtol=1e-4，边界敏感的整数计数允许 ±1。这个部分不是主结果，但对做 SAE 或小模型复现实验的人很实用。28:Taa1,The technical program has four layers, all built around separating geometric recovery from causal validity.

First, the authors construct a fully controlled ground-truth superposition model. It is a small synthetic model trained from scratch, with no pretrained weights and no external data. The point is not realism but identifiability: true feature directions, sparsity, hidden dimensionality, and superposition geometry are all known, so both recovery and intervention can be evaluated rigorously.

Second, they reproduce and refine prior geometric results. The paper reproduces the Elhage et al. superposition phase diagram with multi-seed statistics and identifies an important artifact: at high sparsity, insufficient training can masquerade as superposition structure. This is corrected with sparsity-aware step budgets. The paper also describes a less-discussed diffuse sharing regime at extreme overcompleteness, where every feature is substantially represented while each feature occupies only a small effective dimensional share.

Third, the paper reproduces the TopK-vs-L1 SAE comparison and turns the usual claim about L1 shrinkage into a direct measurement. Instead of asserting that L1 regularization distorts activations, the authors run an activation-refinement test that quantifies the shrinkage. This is important because similar-looking decoder recovery can coexist with very different encoder behavior, which later shows up as causal mismatch.

Fourth, the paper introduces a causal validation battery and a taxonomy of inertness. For every correlationally recovered feature, the authors run ablation and sign-correct steering interventions and propagate the effect through the model’s actual output stage rather than stopping at the SAE reconstruction space. This leads to two orthogonal taxonomies. By cause, there is structural inertness and competitive inertness. Structural inertness arises systematically from antipodal-pair superposition geometry combined with a positive-pass encoder, and better SAE training does not necessarily remove it. Competitive inertness is a TopK competition pathology in degraded dictionaries and is tied to numerical boundary instability. By direction, there is read-inertness and write-inertness: ablation can fail to reveal a feature even when steering can affect it, and steering can fail even when ablation suggests relevance. Antipodal pairs can fully dissociate these two notions.

The work is also packaged into a model-agnostic auditing tool, sae-causal-audit. Any object that can encode, decode, and expose its dictionary can be audited, including sae_lens.SAE. The interface is designed to make a documented class of sign-handling bugs hard to express.29:T9ab,The empirical section is unusually rich and several results are numerically sharp.

First, the superposition phase diagram is reproduced with multi-seed statistics rather than a single run. The paper shows that high-sparsity regions suffer from a convergence artifact: under-training can look like a geometric phase transition. This is fixed with sparsity-aware training budgets. The newly described diffuse sharing regime is quantified by values such as ||W_i||^2 ≈ 1.40 and feature dimensionality D_i ≈ 0.08 under extreme overcompleteness, indicating that features are broadly represented but each occupies only a small dimensional share.

Second, in the TopK-vs-L1 reproduction, the paper directly measures activation refinement and confirms L1 shrinkage rather than treating it as folklore. The excerpt provided here does not include the full Pareto tables, but the qualitative conclusion is clear: decoder alignment alone overstates recovery quality when encoder activations are distorted by shrinkage.

Third, and most importantly, causal validation reveals a strong gap between correlational recovery and causal efficacy. In a degraded SAE, 17 of 22 matched features are causally inert. In a well-trained SAE, only 2 of 22 matched features are inert. More strikingly, inert matches can have cosine similarity as high as 0.9998. This is the paper’s strongest result: near-perfect geometric recovery does not guarantee causal usefulness.

Fourth, the authors run a first small-scale audit on a real published SAE, gpt2-small-res-jb. Against 83 hand-authored concepts spanning unrelated semantic domains, they recover 7 correlational matches, of which 1, or 14%, is causally inert. The sample is small, but the direction is consistent with the toy setting. They also report an unexpected atom-collision phenomenon: a small number of decoder atoms repeatedly appear as nearest matches for dozens of semantically unrelated concepts. This is replicated across three independently constructed concept batches of increasing size and diversity, and a controlled negative case argues against a prompt-template artifact.

Fifth, the reproducibility section explains why byte-exact reproducibility across platforms is unavailable for floating-point training workloads. The paper proposes a two-tier guarantee: SHA-256 byte exactness within one pinned environment, and semantic equality elsewhere with rtol = 1e-4 and boundary-sensitive integer counts allowed to vary by ±1.2a:T755,This is worth a careful read. Start with the central claim and keep it in mind throughout: decoder geometry and causal validity are different empirical statements. Once that is clear, the paper’s structure becomes straightforward.

On a first pass, read the Introduction and the contribution list to see that this is not just another toy SAE reproduction. Then jump directly to the causal validation section, especially the ablation and sign-correct steering protocol. Focus on how interventions are propagated to the model’s actual output layer and how inertness is operationally defined. That is the conceptual center of the paper.

Next, go back to the superposition phase-diagram reproduction. Pay attention to how the high-sparsity convergence artifact is diagnosed, how training budgets are adjusted with sparsity, and what characterizes the diffuse sharing regime. This helps explain why geometrically recovered features can still fail at the encoder or competition stage.

Then read the TopK-vs-L1 reproduction and the activation-refinement test. The key question there is not simply which SAE reconstructs better, but which one recovers features that remain causally actionable. If you usually look only at reconstruction, sparsity, or cosine metrics, this section fills in the missing encoder side.

After that, read the taxonomy and tooling sections. Structural vs competitive inertness and read- vs write-inertness are useful concepts because they turn intervention failure into something diagnosable. Finish with the real-model audit on gpt2-small-res-jb and the atom-collision finding to judge whether the toy conclusions already have echoes in practical SAEs.

If time is limited, prioritize Sections 6, 7, 10, and 11, then return to Sections 4 and 5. If you plan to reproduce the work, Section 8 on reproducibility conventions is also worth close attention.2b:T6ee,The paper does not propose a new confidence estimator. Instead, it takes the most basic available signal—the logit difference between candidate answers—and tests it against a normative theory. Under SDC, confidence is the posterior probability that the chosen answer is correct, conditioned on a latent decision variable that encodes noisy task-relevant evidence. If an observable signal is a monotonic readout of that latent variable, then it carries the information needed, in principle, to compute posterior confidence.

Using this framework, the authors derive a set of qualitative signatures that LD should satisfy if it behaves like a readout of the latent decision variable. The visible text explicitly mentions the psychometric function and, most importantly, the correct/error folded-X pattern. The folded-X signature is especially diagnostic: as stimulus evidence becomes stronger, confidence should rise on correct trials but fall on error trials, producing the characteristic folded X. A generic preference score need not show this structure.

The experiments are built around tasks with an explicit stimulus-strength axis, so that the normative process model is well defined. The paper studies three perceptual discrimination tasks and one memory-based decision task, using three multimodal non-reasoning models and one multimodal reasoning model. This setup lets the authors jointly analyze stimulus strength, model choice, LD magnitude, and correctness. They also add an abstention setting: when the model is allowed to withhold low-confidence answers, does LD support near-optimal selective prediction? That behavioral test is important because it asks not only whether LD correlates with correctness, but whether it can drive sensible decision policies.2c:T553,The main result is that on simple perceptual and memory decision tasks, LD satisfies the key SDC signatures, including the correct/error folded-X pattern. That is the strongest evidence in the paper that answer logits are acting as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than as arbitrary preference scores. In those settings, the confidence signal appears to have a meaningful computational interpretation.

The abstention experiments strengthen that claim. When allowed to abstain, the models preferentially withhold low-LD cases, and the accuracy of committed responses rises to near ceiling. The point is not merely that abstention helps; it is that LD appears behaviorally useful in a way that is close to normative selective prediction.

The paper is also careful about failure modes. In complex visual reasoning, LD still predicts correctness even after accounting for objective task difficulty, so it remains an informative signal. But the full geometric signatures predicted by SDC no longer hold. That suggests the latent-variable story becomes incomplete once tasks no longer admit a simple normative process model or when reasoning is not well captured by a one-dimensional evidence variable. The emphasis is therefore on qualitative structure and behavioral consequences rather than on a single calibration number or headline metric.2d:Ta62,方法上延续前作的设置：在一层 transformer 上做 (a+b) mod 97，主任务损失之外，在最终位置表示上加入 supervised contrastive loss。关键不是又加了一个辅助 loss，而是作者系统构造了几类“只改先验、不改其表面强度”的对照，去分辨先验到底通过什么机制起作用。

第一组是 content control，也就是 band structure。作者把正样本定义为共享 magnitude-band pair 的样本，例如按 ⌊a/10⌋、⌊b/10⌋ 分桶，得到 100 个类，类大小与真实等价类接近。这个先验是连贯的、可学习的，也不是随机噪声；但它依赖的是阈值/幅值类特征，而任务真正可泛化的 Fourier 电路依赖周期性特征。这样就能区分两种解释：如果“只要先验有结构、可学习”就行，band prior 应该像 sibling prior 一样有帮助；如果关键在 feature family 对齐，band prior 就会像随机分区一样阻碍泛化。实验支持后者。

第二组是 supervision control，也就是 commutativity prior。这里完全不用答案标签，只把 (a,b) 和 (b,a) 当作正样本。它只利用加法交换律这个任务家族先验，因此是 label-free invariance prior。这个设计很巧，因为它不是直接告诉模型“哪些样本同类”，而是给出一个稀疏的不变性约束；在 modular addition 里，交换后的样本确实共享同一个和，所以它是对真实等价结构的稀疏采样。作者进一步指出，稀疏性可能反而是优点：它提供方向正确但优化压力更温和的结构信号，减轻前作里观察到的 weight norm inflation 副作用。

第三组是 critical-window 实验。作者只在特定 epoch 区间打开先验损失，比较早期开、晚期开、全程开等设置。结论是先验必须在早期介入，才能改变后续表示形成轨迹；如果模型已经沿着不利路径进入记忆化盆地，后加先验很难把它拉回来。这一点和很多预训练中的“早期数据/目标决定后续表征几何”很接近。

第四组是 generality sweep。作者把分析扩展到 multiplication、subtraction、parity，不同架构变体（2-layer、MLP、LayerNorm 变体），并做 norm clamp sweep，测量前作提出的 delay-law exponent 在有无先验时如何变化。这里的重点不是追求统一提升，而是检验“表征先验 + 权重范数副作用”这个解释是不是稳健。文章还加了一个 sequence-task pilot，作为边界测试，说明这套机制目前更像是对算法任务和小模型训练动力学的精细解释，而不是已经覆盖一般序列建模。2e:Ta22,实验最有信息量的结果有几组。

1. feature family 决定先验是否有效。band prior 15 次里只有 1 次出现 grokking，和 random partition 的 0/20 在统计上不可区分（Fisher exact p=0.43），但与 true structure 的 22/30 显著不同（p=2.3×10^-5）。这说明“结构连贯、类别大小匹配、可学习”都不够，先验若落在错误的特征族上，效果接近噪声结构。对照地，前作里的 sibling structure 虽然组合关系不对，但仍共享同一特征族，因此 14/15 能泛化。这个对照是全文最关键的证据。

2. 标签不是必要条件。commutativity prior 在 15/15 个 run 中全部泛化，而 random partition 是 0/20，显著性很强（p=3.1×10^-10）。相对同 seed baseline，中位数加速 2.7×。更有意思的是，它的成功率还高于使用标签定义真实等价类的 true-structure prior（22/30，二者比较 p=0.038）。作者给出的解释是：commutativity prior 更稀疏，每个被覆盖 anchor 只有一个正样本，因而提供了正确方向的几何约束，但不会像密集监督那样过度推高权重范数。

3. norm side-effect 仍然存在，但减弱。文中报告 commutativity arms 的 median peak weight norm 为 103，低于 supervised true-structure prior 的 130，但仍高于 baseline 的约 57。这与前作一致：辅助对比损失一方面加快结构形成，另一方面也会推高权重范数，延缓优化；最终效果取决于两者博弈。仅看最终 held norm 不够，轨迹更重要。

4. 与 norm clamp 结合后效果最好。把 label-free commutativity prior 与前作提出的 norm clamp（||W||=45）结合，5/5 run 全部 grok，且中位数加速达到 17×；有些 seed 在 1000 epoch 以内就完成泛化。这个结果说明，先验提供方向，norm control 去掉副作用，两者是可组合的。

5. critical window。虽然给出的全文片段没有列出全部表格数字，但作者明确报告：只在训练早期施加先验仍能保留大部分收益，而只在后期施加几乎不起作用。这不是简单的“多训练一段辅助 loss 就行”，而是表示几何在早期被锁定，后期更像是在已有表征上做微调。

6. 泛化范围。作者在 multiplication、subtraction、parity，以及 2-layer、MLP、LayerNorm 变体上做了扩展，结论是“feature-family alignment + early window + norm interaction”这套解释有一定稳健性，但不是无条件普适。sequence-task pilot 被明确当作边界探测，说明作者对外推范围是克制的。2f:T8f7,The setup follows the companion paper: a one-layer transformer trained on modular addition, with the main task loss plus a supervised contrastive loss applied to the final-position representation. The key contribution is not the auxiliary loss itself, but a carefully designed set of controls that isolate why a prior works.

The first control is the band-structure prior. Positive pairs are defined by sharing the same magnitude-band pair, using bucketed values of a and b. This prior is coherent, learnable, and class-balanced relative to the true task structure. But it is built from threshold/magnitude features, whereas the known generalizing circuit for modular arithmetic relies on periodic/Fourier features. This lets the authors separate two hypotheses: if coherence alone is enough, the band prior should help; if feature-family alignment is necessary, it should behave like a random partition. The experiments support the latter.

The second control is the commutativity prior. Positive pairs are only commuted inputs, (a,b) and (b,a). This uses no answer labels at all. It injects a task-family invariance that is knowable before training and, in modular addition, corresponds to a sparse sample of the true equivalence structure because commuted pairs share the same sum. The design is important because it shows that the useful signal need not be dense label supervision; a weak but correctly aligned invariance can be sufficient.

The third component is the critical-window intervention. The prior is activated only during selected epoch ranges. This tests whether the prior changes optimization globally or whether it must shape representation geometry before the model settles into a memorization-dominated trajectory. The reported result is strongly asymmetric: early intervention works, late intervention mostly does not.

The fourth component is a generality sweep across tasks, architectures, and normalization settings, including multiplication, subtraction, parity, 2-layer variants, MLPs, and LayerNorm variants. The paper also revisits the norm-clamp mechanism from the companion work and measures how the delay-law exponent changes with and without the prior. An exploratory sequence-task pilot is included as a boundary check rather than a claim of broad universality.30:T9a6,Several results carry most of the paper’s weight.

1. Feature-family alignment, not mere coherence, determines usefulness. The band prior yields grokking in only 1 of 15 runs, statistically indistinguishable from a random partition baseline at 0 of 20 runs (Fisher exact p=0.43), and clearly different from the true-structure prior at 22 of 30 runs (p=2.3e-5). This is the cleanest evidence in the paper: a prior can be structured, learnable, and class-size matched, yet still fail if it is expressed in the wrong feature family.

2. Labels are not required. The commutativity prior generalizes in 15 of 15 runs, versus 0 of 20 for the random partition (p=3.1e-10), with a median 2.7x speedup over same-seed baselines. More interestingly, it is more reliable than the label-supervised true-structure prior itself (22 of 30; comparison p=0.038). The authors argue that sparsity is likely the reason: one positive per covered anchor gives the right geometric direction with gentler optimization pressure.

3. The norm side effect remains but is reduced. Median peak weight norm is reported as 103 for the commutativity prior, compared with 130 for the supervised true-structure prior and about 57 for the baseline. This supports the companion paper’s picture: the auxiliary prior helps representation formation but can also inflate weight norms, which delays or destabilizes generalization. The trajectory matters more than a single final norm value.

4. Combining the label-free prior with norm control gives the strongest result. With the norm clamp at ||W||=45, the commutativity prior achieves 5 of 5 successful grokking runs and a median 17x speedup, with some seeds generalizing in under 1,000 epochs. This is a strong demonstration that the useful structural signal and the harmful optimization side effect are separable and composable.

5. Critical-window behavior is clear. Even though the excerpt does not include every table, the paper states that early-only application preserves most of the benefit, while late-only application is largely ineffective. That points to path dependence in representation formation rather than a simple cumulative regularization effect.

6. Generality is partial, not unlimited. The same qualitative account appears across several arithmetic tasks and architecture variants, but the sequence-task pilot is framed as exploratory. The paper is careful not to overclaim beyond the regime where the mechanism is directly testable.31:T540,Start with the Introduction. It lays out the four axes—content, supervision, timing, and generality—and makes clear that the paper is testing the necessity and boundary conditions of the companion paper’s causal story.

Then focus on the two central experiments: band prior and commutativity prior. The band prior explains why a structured prior can still be useless if it is built from the wrong features. The commutativity prior explains why labels are not essential if the invariance is aligned with the task. If figures are available, prioritize success-rate tables, time-to-generalization distributions, and weight-norm trajectories.

Next, read the critical-window and norm-clamp sections together. That is where the paper becomes most relevant to pretraining practice: the prior is not just accelerating optimization in a generic sense, it is shaping early representation geometry, while norm control removes a specific side effect.

For a deeper read, it helps a lot to read the companion paper alongside this one. Many design choices—same-seed pairing, censored-run handling, matched class sizes, and weight-norm-matched controls—matter because the claims are mechanistic, not just empirical. geometricall","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-14T05:21:38Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2607_12360.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文回答一个对 LLM 预训练很实际的问题：WSD 学习率日程里的 cooldown 为什么有时能降低最终 loss，有时几乎没有效果。关键结论是，答案不只取决于 loss landscape 或噪声大小，而取决于两件事的组合：梯度噪声是乘性还是加性，以及优化器的 update magnitude 是否会随着梯度变小而自然变小。","importance":"这是 must-read，因为它把 cooldown 从经验 recipe 拆成了可验证的机制。LLM 预训练中常用 Adam、AdamW、Adafactor 等自适应方法，这类方法在很多分析里可近似为 normalized 或 sign-like update：方向来自梯度，步长幅度不完全随梯度范数收缩。论文说明，在纯乘性噪声下，SGD 会 self-anneal，固定学习率已经能几何收敛；但 normalized 方法不会 self-anneal，会停在 Θ(η²) 的 last-iterate loss floor，因此需要 cooldown 把 η 降下来。这个结论不能直接给出大模型最佳 cooldown fraction，但它解释了为什么同一 loss、同一噪声下，换优化器会改变 cooldown 是否有用。对预训练实验设计的影响是明确的：分析 schedule 时不能只看 learning rate curve，也要测量噪声结构和 update normalization。","technical_approach":"论文从 separable strongly convex quadratic 入手，使用 affine-variance 噪声模型：梯度噪声方差由 σ0² + σ1²||∇f||² 控制。σ0 是加性噪声项，在 optimum 处仍存在；σ1 是乘性噪声项，随梯度一起消失。对于 σ0=0 的乘性噪声，SGD 的二阶矩满足完全乘性递推：m_{t+1}=r(η)m_t，其中 r(η)=(1-ηλ)²+η²σ1²λ²。最优固定步长是 η*=1/(λ(1+σ1²))，此时收缩因子为 σ1²/(1+σ1²)，固定学习率就是固定 horizon 下的最优 schedule，WSD cooldown fraction 的最优值为 0。相反，对 signSGD 或 normalized methods，乘性噪声下“走对方向”的概率与当前位置尺度无关。即使靠近 minimizer，更新步长仍由 η 控制，而不会随梯度范数自然缩小，所以动力学变成可解析的 lattice walk，并出现 Θ(η²) 的 last-iterate loss floor。只要存在加性噪声 σ0>0，所有方法都会出现 η 相关的 floor。论文还把结论扩展到局部 (L0,L1)-smooth setting、多维 normalized SGD、momentum、以及 heavy-tailed 噪声；其中 heavy-tail 部分强调分类不依赖有限矩假设。","experiments":"实验主要验证理论预测，而不是追求大规模预训练结果。论文报告了多组 simulation，包括 quadratic 设置和 nonconvex deep linear iterate；加入加性噪声后，SGD 也会出现需要小 η 才能压低的噪声 floor。论文还在真实分类任务上直接测量噪声结构和 schedule preference，并用 Figure 1 和 Figure 2 展示预测与观测的一致性。需要注意，文中没有声称这些实验复现 LLM 预训练尺度，而是把它们作为机制验证。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 2 和相关 remark 是 normalized methods 的核心，重点看为什么 correct-step probability 与 iterate scale 无关，以及为什么这会产生 Θ(η²) floor。Prop. 3 用来理解加性噪声如何改变所有方法的结论。Section 6–9 可按需读，重点是哪些假设被放宽：非二次局部光滑、多维、momentum、heavy cooldown phase in a warmup-stable-decay schedule actually improves the final iterate. The answer depends on a joint condition: the structure of gradient noise and whether the optimizer’s update magnitude shrinks with the gradient. Holding the loss and the noise fixed, changing the optimizer can flip cooldown from unnecessary to useful.","importance":"This is a high-value paper for pretraining research because it gives a mechanistic account of a schedule choice that is now common in large-model training. The result is especially relevant to Adam-like optimizers, which often behave more like normalized or sign-based methods than plain SGD in simplified analyses. Under purely multiplicative noise, SGD self-anneals and a constant learning rate is already optimal for the last iterate. Normalized methods do not self-anneal; they hit a last-iterate loss floor of order η² and need cooldown to lower that floor. The paper does not solve the optimal cooldown fraction for LLM-scale training, but it gives a concrete diagnostic: schedule effects should be analyzed together with noise structure and optimizer normalization, not from the learning-rate curve alone.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"The experiments are designed to test the mechanism rather than to train large language models. Simulations on the quadratic setting verify the quantitative predictions for SGD and normalized/sign-like methods. WSD sweeps on a nonconvex deep linear network reproduce the optimizer-dependent schedule preference: SGD under multiplicative noise does not benefit from cooldown, while normalized updates do because their last-iterate loss is limited by a step-size floor. Adding an additive noise component restores a floor for all methods. The paper also measures noise structure and schedule preference directly on a real classification task, with the key observations shown in Figures 1 and 2. The empirical section supports the theory’s qualitative split, while leaving LLM-scale cooldown length and nonstationary training effects outside its claims.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. Start with the introduction’s framing of cooldown as a last-iterate effect, since this explains why min-over-iterates analyses miss the phenomenon. Then read the setup and noise model to separate additive noise from multiplicative noise. Proposition 1 is the central result for SGD: focus on the multiplicative second-moment recursion and the derivation of η*. Lemma 1 and Proposition 2 are the central results for normalized methods: focus on the scale-invariance of the correct-step probability and the resulting Θ(η²) floor. Proposition 3 explains why additive noise changes the conclusion for all optimizers. Sections 6–9 are useful for checking which assumptions are essential and which are not. Section 10 is worth reading with the figures open. We introduce \\emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \\[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \\qquad Λ_t=\\diag(α_t\\odot e^{iθ_t}). \\] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factoriza","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T17:31:03Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_11897.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决的是线性注意力中的固定状态记忆问题：KDA 这类 delta-rule 层推理高效，但纯实数对角衰减对精确复制、周期状态跟踪、算法记忆和长程检索并不友好。作者的问题不是简单加入更多参数，而是在 KDA 风格的 recurrent memory 中加入可控相位动态，同时不破坏 chunkwise WY kernel 的结构和并行计算性。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在架构与长上下文效率方向。SFDA 试图扩展 KDA 的表达能力，使线性注意力层能承担更多状态跟踪和算法记忆，从而减少全局 softmax attention 的占比。作者明确把系统目标设为测试更高的 linear-to-global attention ratio，例如 7:1 或 15:1，而不是 KDA 风格 3:1 混合比例。当前贡献更偏理论和 kernel 设计，还没有大规模 LM 训练结果，因此影响量级取决于后续能否在真实预训练中保持稳定性、吞吐和困惑度收益。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention。它在 KDA 的 diagonal decay 加 rank-one delta correction 之外，引入按 2×2 block 对齐的旋转控制矩阵。实数坐标下，每个状态块先经过旋转 U_t 和正衰减 D_t；映射到复数坐标后，这一操作等价于复对角矩阵 Λ_t 的 phase-decay。单步转移保持为 A_t = Λ_t - u_t w_t^*，也就是相位衰减加一个 rank-one 修正。KDA 是 SFDA 的特例：把相位设为 0，或令 U_t = I。关键取舍在于作者没有使用 dense phase/control matrix，因为那会把 chunk 内核退化成密集 d×d 乘积，失去线性注意力的硬件友好性。论文的主要技术结果是 constructive left-to-right chunk-WY theorem：对 P_t = A_t A_{t-1} ... A_1，显式递推 Γ_t、Y_t、M_t、W_t 等因子，使 rank 只在固定 chunk 内增长，而不是随全序列增长。作者还给出 exact affine chunk transfer，包括 additive write summary 的计算方式；并讨论 tied SFDA、write-decoupled variant、复数到实数实现、代价界、数值稳定性和 rank-growth discipline。表达能力部分用 automata 语言说明 SFDA 可以紧凑实现周期计数器、dihedral phase-orientation memory、register/reset memory、bounded stack 等结构；同时证明 generalized tied-write affine memory system 在 one-hot lifting 后可以精确实现任意确定性有限自动机。","experiments":"这篇更像理论和 kernel 预案，提供的是构造性证明、复杂度分析和稳定性讨论，而不是完整的语言模型实验。文中没有给出大规模预训练困惑度、下游 benchmark 或真实长上下文检索结果。可提取的具体实验目标是系统层设想：用更强的 SFDA 线性层支持 7:1 或 15:1 的 linear-to-global attention ratio，并与 KDA 风格 3:1 hybrid 在等算力下比较。当前结论只能说明该结构在代数闭包、chunk 化计算和若干自动机任务表达上可行，不能说明它已经在真实 LM 训练中优于 KDA 或 softmax attention。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 到 45 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中对 KDA 局限和 SFDA 设计约束的说明，理解为什么必须限制为 aligned 2×2 rotation-decay block。然后看 constructive chunk-WY theorem，这是全篇最关键的 kernel 结果，重点关注 rank 只在 chunk 内增长的递推结构。再看 affine chunk transfer 和 additive write summary，它们决定这个层能否用于实际并行训练。表达能力章节可以选择性阅读，重点看 cyclic counters、register/reset memory 和 finite automata realization；这些部分有助于判断相位控制是否真的补足了 KDA 的算法状态短板。复数到实数实现、cost bounds 和 numerical stability lemma 值得做工程实现前细读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific weakness of fixed-state linear attention. KDA-style delta memory is efficient at inference time, but a purely real diagonal decay is not a natural mechanism for exact copying, cyclic state tracking, algorithmic memory, or long-context retrieval. The goal is to add phase-controlled dynamics to KDA-like recurrent memory while preserving the structured chunk kernel that makes the layer practical.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining is architectural. We introduce Support Vector Attention (SV-Attention), a max-margin memory whose weights are support coefficients of a one-class SVM with fixed box parameter C. Its active-set partition gives reserve tokens exactly zero weight, certifying output-preserving eviction; a reversible incremental solver deletes a token","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-13T23:13:57Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_12204.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 softmax attention 与线性 recurrent memory 的设计空间：让记忆天然稀疏，并把可删性变成结构性结果。限制也很明显：精确路径依赖 fp64 和 SVM active-set solver；用于训练的 batched path 是近似可微版本，不再提供严格删除证书，离大规模 LLM 训练还有距离。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Support Vector Attention。它把上下文 key 输入到 one-class SVM，attention 权重不是 softmax 相似度，而是 SVM 的 support coefficients。active-set partition 把 token 分成 reserve、margin support、error/bound support 等类别；reserve token 的系数严格为 0，因此可以删除且输出不变。精确 solver 采用 Cauwenberghs 和 Poggio 的增量/减量 SVM 算法，维护 margin support vectors 上的 bordered KKT matrix inverse。这个矩阵同时服务三个功能：前向求解 active set，证明 reserve token 无贡献，以及通过 implicit function theorem 做自定义反向传播。论文还区分了两条数值路径：fp64 maintained solver 用于精确选择和精确 forgetting；batched training path 用近似 partition 加 1e-3 ridge 的 torch.linalg.solve 做可微训练，但它的系数只是稳定化近似，不是 exact-deletion certificate。","experiments":"精确 forgetting 实验把 decremental deletion 与“删掉该点后从头 refit”的结果比较。在 Gaussian、MIMIC-IV 真实医疗流、以及训练模型产生的 learned keys 上，只要 optimum 唯一，support-vector partition 的恢复率为 98%–100%。decision function 与 refit 的 median deviation 约为 1e-9，在 learned keys 上约为 1e-13；最差 1.1e-2 出现在病态 near-duplicate streams 中，仍低于 coefficient decay 的偏差。选择实验中，在 skewed redundancy 下，certified selection 在相同 token budget 下的 rare-item recall 为 0.86，而给 H2O-style heavy-hitter eviction 提供 oracle evaluation queries 后仍只有 0.32。adaptive support set 在 40 个 distinct items 时保持 0.95 recall，而 matched-state DeltaNet recurrence 降到 0.06。真实数据演示包括 learned keys 上的 surgical fact forgetting、knowledge editing，以及 MIMIC-IV patient deletion；文中报告 zero leakage，并在 critical hours retention 上得到 0.80 vs. 0.05 的对比。训练效率方面，batched path 在 full-model benchmark 中达到 9,125 tokens/s，但比 unfused PyTorch CPU softmax 慢 6.1 倍，比 MPS softmax reference 慢 35.8 倍。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。重点看 Section 3 的 看训练路径的效率和证书失效边界。需要特别留意论文的边界：精确性属于 fp64 maintained solver；训练时的 batched differentiable approximation 主要证明可训练性，不等价于可部署的大规模高效 attention 替代品。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets two missing capabilities in test-time memories: identifying context tokens that can be evicted without changing the output, and deleting the influence of a specific token exactly. Existing attention-like memories, linear recurrent memories, DeltaNet-style updates, mesa layers, Titans, and SSM-style states are mostly dense. Tokens may decay, but they rarely become provably irrelevant.","importance":"The main value is architectural. It does not yet provide a scalable recipe for LLM pretraining, but it opens a clean design point for long-context memory: make sparsity and removability part of the inner learning problem rather than post-hoc cache heuristics. The contribution is strongest as a conceptual and numerical prototype. The exact guarantees rely on an fp64 active-set SVM solver, while the trainable batched path is only an approximate differentiable surrogate and does not retain the exact deletion certificate.","technical_approach":"Support Vector Attention treats attention as a one-class SVM over keys. The attention weights are support coefficients rather than softmax scores. The active-set partition gives reserve tokens exactly zero coefficient, so evicting them is output-preserving by complementary slackness. For exact updates and deletions, the method uses the incremental and decremental SVM algorithm of Cauwenberghs and Poggio, maintaining the inverse of the bordered KKT matrix over margin support vectors. That maintained inverse is reused for the forward solve, for the selection certificate, and for a custom vector-Jacobian product via implicit differentiation. The implementation separates the exact fp64 maintained solver from a batched training path, which finds approximate partitions and solves a ridged linear system with standard autograd.","experiments":"$19","reading_guide":"Skim in 25–35 minutes. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-14T14:02:22Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_12789.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是高效注意力里的一个具体问题：VQ-attention 虽然把复杂度从 O(N^2) 降到 O(MN)，但它对整个 key 空间使用固定、均匀的码本容量，导致高注意力区域量化过粗、低注意力区域又浪费表示预算。作者要做的是让码本容量随输入和注意力分布自适应分配，在不回到二次复杂度的前提下，把算力集中到真正重要的区域。","importance":"对 LLM 基座预训练的直接相关性有限，因为全文实验主要在图像分类、分割和扩散模型上，没有给出大语言模型预训练或长上下文语言建模结果。但它对架构和高效注意力研究有两个启发。第一，线性/近线性注意力不一定只能靠固定稀疏模式或核近似，也可以从“可学习量化 + 输入自适应细化”这个方向做。第二，作者把算法设计和 FlashAttention 风格实现绑得很紧，说明高效注意力若想真正可用，不能只给理论复杂度，还要给 tile-level、kernel-level 的实现路径。对做长上下文 LLM 的人，这篇更像是可借鉴的思路储备，而不是可以直接迁移的 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法核心是分层向量量化注意力。标准 VQ-attention 先把 key 聚到 M 个 codeword，再按簇聚合 value，从而把 query-key 交互从 N 个 key 压到 M 个代表点。问题在于 M 是固定的，所有区域精度一样。AVQ 的做法是先用一层较粗的 parent codeword 做近似，再根据当前输入前向传播中得到的注意力质量分布，选出最重要的若干 parent，对这些区域展开更细的 child codeword。这样，高注意力区域得到更细量化，低注意力区域维持粗粒度表示。\n\n这里真正有技术含量的部分有三处。第一，importance signal 的定义不需要额外学习，直接来自前向中的 attention mass。也就是看哪些 codeword 承接了更多注意力，再决定细化谁。第二，作者设计了层级码本的几何约束：每个 parent 等于其 children 的均值。这个约束不是装饰性的，它让 parent 的贡献可以从 child logits 中恢复出来，从而在 refinement 时不必重新访问 parent codeword。第三，作者把这个过程嵌进 FlashAttention 的 tiled / online softmax 计算框架里。因为 FlashAttention 不会显式物化完整注意力矩阵，importance 统计和后续修正都必须在 tile 级别、寄存器级别完成。论文给出的方案是先算 parent-level attention，再对 top-P parent 逐块引入 child block，并通过在线 softmax 的增量更新和 parent-child 约束做 in-register correction。\n\n复杂度上，作者声称维持与标准 VQ-attention 相同的 O(MN)，只是把固定预算改成自适应预算分配。实现上还提供了 Triton kernel，把量化、聚合和修正过程尽量融合，减少 memory traffic。这一点很重要，因为很多高效注意力方法理论上更省，但 wall-clock 并不占优。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ImageNet-1k 分类、ADE20K 语义分割和 Stable Diffusion 高分辨率生成。结论比较一致：在相近计算预算下，AVQ-attention 比固定码本的 VQ-attention 有更好的精度-效率折中，并且和一些现有高效注意力方法有竞争力。论文还强调一个实用点：VQ/AVQ-attention 可以后置应用到预训练 transformer，再用少量 epoch 微调，类似量化感知训练后的压缩流程，而不是必须从头训练。\n\n从全文可提炼的实验重点不是单个任务分数，而是三类验证。第一类是算法有效性：自适应细化是否优于平坦码本。第二类是实现有效性：自定义 Triton kernel 是否把理论收益转成 wall-clock 改善。第三类是迁移方式：post-hoc 替换后经过短时微调能否恢复甚至提升性能。对基座研究者来说，第三点最有现实意义，因为它关系到高效注意力能否在已有大模型上低成本试验。\n\n不过也要明确边界。论文没有在 decoder-only LLM、长上下文困境、KV-cache 场景或预训练吞吐上给出证据，因此不能直接推断它对大语言模型训练/推理一定有效。VQ 聚类、码本学习和动态细化在自回归语言建模里是否稳定，还需要单独验证。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按“问题定义 → 算法机制 → 实现细节 → 实验结论”四步看。先看 Introduction，明确它相对固定 VQ-attention 多解决了什么。然后重点看方法部分里 parent-child 层级码本、importance 计算、以及 parent 等于 children 均值这个约束，这是整套方法能高效实现的关键。接着看和 FlashAttention 结合的那部分，理解为什么完整注意力矩阵不落地会让自适应 refinement 变难，以及作者如何用 online softmax 的增量形式绕过去。最后看 wall-clock 和 post-hoc fine-tuning 结果。\n\n如果你做 LLM 长上下文或高效推理，这篇适合 30 到 45 分钟泛读。值得深挖的点有两个：一是这种“先粗后细”的量化注意力是否能迁移到 decoder-only causal attention；二是动态细化带来的控制流和 kernel 复杂度，是否会在真实 serving 场景里抵消理论收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific weakness of vector-quantized attention. Standard VQ-attention reduces complexity from O(N^2) to O(MN) by replacing keys with M codewords, but it allocates codebook capacity uniformly across key space. That means high-attention regions may be represented too coarsely, while low-attention regions receive unnecessary precision. AVQ-Attention tries to make this allocation input-adaptive, concentrating compute where attention mass actually falls.","importance":"Its direct relevance to LLM pretraining is limited, since the experiments are on vision backbones and diffusion models rather than decoder-only language models. First, efficient attention does not have to rely only on fixed sparsity or kernel approximations; learned quantization with adaptive refinement is another viable direction. Second, the paper treats algorithm design and systems implementation together. That matters because many efficient-attention proposals look good asymptotically but fail to improve wall-clock performance. For long-context LLM work, this is more of a design reference than a ready-to-use recipe.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"A good skim order is: problem setup, algorithm, implementation, then experiments. In this work, we exploit the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional space","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-13T03:53:56Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_11976.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文稀疏注意力里的一个很具体但很卡脖子的内核问题：Indexer-TopK。它既要算 query 对大量候选 key 的相关性分数，又要做逐行 top-k 选择。现有 GPU 实现通常把“算分”和“选 top-k”拆成两步，先把完整 score matrix 写回 HBM，再做选择。这样做在 1M 级上下文下会同时带来三类代价：显存占用高、HBM 读写流量大、同步和选择开销重。论文指出，在 DeepSeek Sparse Attention 这类系统里，这个内核已经不是次要细节，而是 prefill 的主瓶颈；文中给出的例子是 GLM-5.2 在 1M context 下，DSA kernel 占到 prefill 总时长的 83.7%。","importance":"对基座研究的意义不在于提出新的建模方法，而在于把“长上下文稀疏化是否真的省”这个问题往前推了一步。很多稀疏注意力方案在算法层面减少了理论 FLOPs，但工程上会被 score materialization 和 top-k selection 吃掉收益。LiteTopK 的价值是把这个瓶颈重新定义成一个 fused kernel 设计问题，并利用高维相似度分布集中这一统计性质来减少无效写回。它更像 serving / inference kernel 的工作，但对长上下文预训练和推理一体化系统都有启发：如果稀疏注意力的索引阶段不能做成低内存、低 I/O 的 fused primitive，那么很多长上下文 recipe 在真实系统里很难落地。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个 sample-filter-select 框架。出发点不是精确地维护所有候选分数，而是先快速估计“top-k 大概会落在哪个分数区间”，然后在主计算过程中用这个区间阈值尽早过滤掉不可能进入 top-k 的候选。\n\n具体做法分三步。第一步是 sample。作者利用 sparse attention 的局部平稳性：相邻 token chunk 往往关注相近的历史 token，因此当前 chunk 的分数分布和前一个 chunk 相似。LiteTopK 直接拿前一 chunk 的 top-k' 分数作为轻量样本，文中在 DSA 上取 k'=3k，用它估计当前 chunk 的 score range。第二步是 quantize + histogram。把估计出的分数范围做等宽分桶，建立 histogram，定位包含局部 top-k 元素的 threshold bin。第三步是 fused filtering。真正遍历所有候选时，不再把完整 score 写回 HBM，而是边算边根据当前 threshold bin 的上界过滤掉明显不可能进入 top-k 的候选，只对保留下来的少量分数做写回和后续选择。\n\n实现上有几个值得注意的点。其一，作者把“算 score”改写成“直接算 bin id”，尽量不增加额外算子延迟。其二，histogram 更新没有用锁，而是让空闲 warps 通过 overwrite write 式更新，接受轻微陈旧、略松的阈值，因为这不会破坏正确性，只会影响过滤强度。其三，方法依赖的是高维相似度的集中现象：大多数分数挤在一个窄区间里，真正高分是长尾少数，因此阈值一旦建立，过滤率会很高。和传统 exact top-k kernel 相比，它不是先完整 materialize 再 select，而是把近似分布估计嵌进主路径，尽量把无效候选挡在 HBM 之外。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，最关键的实验结论集中在系统瓶颈刻画和分布观察上。论文展示了在 GLM-5.2、1M context 的 prefill 场景里，DSA kernel 占总 runtime 的 83.7%，说明优化空间确实主要在 Indexer-TopK。显存方面，原始 DSA 在 moderate chunk size 8192 时会产生 32 GB 的运行时内存开销，这会直接挤压 KV cache 空间，逼得 vLLM 把 chunk 进一步切成 128 之类的小 sub-chunk，吞吐随之下降。\n\n分布层面，作者给出第一层 DSA score 的例子：分数大多集中在 25 到 35，45 以上极少，在 1M token context 下大约只有 10K 个。这为 sample-filter 设计提供了经验依据。摘要和正文片段表明，论文还做了与现有 GPU Indexer-TopK kernel、尤其是 DSA kernel 的对比，并强调减少 HBM write-back、降低 memory overhead 和 latency；但当前提供的全文片段没有包含完整表格、吞吐倍数、端到端延迟改善百分比、不同 k 和不同 embedding 维度下的曲线，因此无法负责任地补出具体加速数字。若做泛读，先把它当作“利用分布集中做 fused top-k”的 kernel 设计，而不是先记具体 benchmark 数字。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里两个事实：一是 DSA 在 1M prefill 下占 83.7% runtime，二是 score materialization 带来 32 GB 级额外显存。这两点决定了问题是否成立。然后重点看方法部分里 sample-filter-select 的数据流，尤其是为什么可以用前一 chunk 的 top-k' 估计当前 chunk 分布，以及 threshold bin 如何在遍历过程中动态更新。再看实现细节：bin-id 计算替代原始 score 计算、idle warps 更新 histogram、stale threshold 为什么不影响 correctness。最后再看实验里不同场景下的过滤率、HBM traffic、吞吐变化。如果时间有限，30 分钟内抓住问题定义和 kernel 机制即可；如果要判断能否迁移到别的 sparse attention 或 retrieval kernel，需要细看它对分数分布稳定性和 chunk 相似性的依赖。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a very specific systems bottleneck in long-context sparse attention: the Indexer-TopK primitive that computes query-candidate scores and performs row-wise top-k selection. Existing GPU implementations usually decouple scoring from selection: they first materialize the full score matrix in HBM, then run a top-k kernel. At million-token context lengths, this becomes expensive in three ways at once: large runtime memory overhead, heavy HBM traffic, and costly synchronization/selection. The paper argues that in practical sparse-attention deployments this is no longer a secondary issue. In the GLM-5.2 example reported here, the DSA kernel accounts for 83.7% of prefill runtime at 1M context.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model work is not a new modeling paradigm but a sharper answer to a recurring question: does sparse attention remain efficient after the indexing stage is accounted for? Many sparse-attention methods reduce theoretical attention cost, yet lose much of the gain to score materialization and top-k selection. LiteTopK matters because it reframes the bottleneck as a fused kernel design problem and exploits score concentration in high-dimensional similarity search to cut unnecessary write-back. This is mainly an inference/serving paper, but it is still useful for long-context pretraining and deployment research: if the sparse indexing primitive is not memory-light and I/O-efficient, many long-context recipes will not translate into real system gains.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"Start with the Introduction and lock onto two facts: DSA takes 83.7% of runtime in 1M-token prefill, and score materialization adds 32 GB of runtime memory overhead. If time is limited, 30 minutes is enough to understand the problem framing and the kernel mechanism. Existing dynamic caching methods such as TeaCache, EasyCache, and DiCache use a fixed threshold on an accumulated drift signal to decide whether to skip an expensive model evaluation. A fixed threshold either caches too aggressively at sensitive denoising steps and hurts quality, or stays conservative and leaves speedup on the table.","importance":"The work is only indirectly related to LLM pretraining, but it is relevant for diffusion-based multimodal generation and serving. The useful lesson is that denoising steps are not uniformly important. Some timesteps are critical because the model’s intermediate signal changes rapidly, and aggressive reuse at those points has a disproportionate quality cost. This view may transfer to dynamic compute allocation in diffusion LMs, unified AR-diffusion systems, video generation pipelines, and feature-cache scheduling.","technical_approach":"ACID keeps the structure of existing dynamic caching methods but changes the threshold schedule. Standard methods maintain an accumulated drift metric that estimates how much the model output has changed since the last full evaluation. If the drift stays below a user-defined threshold, they reuse cached outputs with lightweight correction; if it exceeds the threshold, they run the full model and reset the drift. ACID replaces the constant threshold with an adaptive one. It monitors the rate of change of the underlying metric and uses a second-derivative-style signal to detect critical steps. It then switches to a lower, more conservative threshold near those steps and a higher, more aggressive threshold elsewhere. Because it operates on top of each method’s own metric, it can wrap TeaCache, EasyCache, and DiCache rather than requiring a new caching algorithm.","experiments":"The paper notes that generating roughly five-second videos can take about 3 to 23 minutes on a single A100, with most latency coming from the sequential denoising backbone. In the TeaCache and HunyuanVideo setting, ACID reaches up to 2.16× speedup over the no-cache baseline and up to 38% additional speedup over a conservative fixed-threshold baseline. Reported quality changes are small: less than 0.3 dB PSNR drop, less than 0.01 SSIM drop, and less than 0.01 LPIPS increase. The main empirical claim is that avoiding aggressive caching at critical steps improves the latency-quality tradeoff compared with any single fixed threshold.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. It separates two questions that are usually collapsed into one number: whether the task truly requires multiple ordered frames, and whether the model recovers that order from visual motion or from positional encodings such as RoPE. Existing checks, including frame-shuffle sensitivity and full-video gain, mostly test the first question and do not identify the channel used by the model.","importance":"The contribution is mainly an evaluation and diagnostic tool for video VLM pretraining research. It shows that temporal benchmark accuracy can overstate visual temporal modeling: a model may answer order-sensitive questions by following position indices rather than the observed visual sequence. The main channel diagnostic is reversal-drop: reverse the visual frame order while keeping RoPE positions forward. This creates a controlled conflict between the pixel sequence and the positional sequence. A large accuracy loss indicates visual-sequence dominance; little or no loss indicates position dominance. pair","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-11T09:30:35Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_11942.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文审计 设置下，五个 backend-comparable 方法中只有 query-independent 的 KeyDiff 稳定超过 best-of-3 trivial baseline，达到 31/36 cells，平均 +0.171；SnapKV 在 agnostic 设置下平均低于该基线，只在 13/36 cells 胜出，平均 -0.066，但在 query-aware 设置可复现 +0.132 的收益。LongBench 上结论更温和：query-agnostic 的有效性仍存在，但不是 KeyDiff 独占，其他方法会追上或超过。两个重要审计发现是：eager 与 sdpa attention backend 混用可让 RULER pooled accuracy 移动 -0.221，幅度超过许多方法差异；RULER-8192 在 gemma-2 tokenizer visibility 改变，是主要结果。Section 4.4 的 selection-bias correction 可以快速判断 baseline 设计是否过于苛刻。Section 5 适合看机制解释：不同方法的 score() 是否直接使用 query，和性能掉落顺序基本一致。Section 6.2 和 6.3 很实用，分别讨论 attention backend 混用和 tokenizer-dependent length overflow，这两点对复现实验和构建长上下文评测都容易踩坑。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper audits a deployment mismatch in KV-cache compression evaluation. Many methods are evaluated in a query-aware protocol, where the question is visible before cache pruning. The economically relevant reuse setting is often query-agnostic: compress a document once, then answer many future questions whose content is unknown at compression time.","importance":"The contribution is mainly an evaluation and systems result. It shows that KV-cache compression rankings can depend strongly on whether the query is visible during pruning. This matters for long-context model research because cache reuse, memory pressure, and prefill amortization shape practical model design. A protocol that lets the compressor see the query can overstate the value of methods that are not useful in a compress-once-query-many deployment.","technical_approach":"The paper performs a matched-budget audit rather than proposing a new compressor. It fixes the model, instances, compression ratio, and decoding, and varies only the compression method and the query-visibility protocol. The audited methods are SnapKV, H2O/ObservedAttention, TOVA, ExpectedAttention, AdaKV, and KeyDiff. The simple baselines are random eviction, key-norm selection, and StreamingLLM-style start-plus-recent retention, with a FullCache anchor. Compression ratios are r ∈ {0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9}. The comparison target is the best of the three trivial baselines within each cell, which is a strong but useful deployment filter. The audit also uses exact per-instance pairing, coverage checks, bootstrap uncertainty, and a correction for best-of-selection bias. Two implementation hazards receive special attention: mixing attention backends and ignoring tokenizer-dependent length overflow.","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"Skim time: 20–30 minutes. Start with the audit setup: the cell definition, the two query-visibility protocols, and the best-of-3 trivial baseline. Section 4.2 contains the main ranking changes. Reasoning。第一层覆盖物体/部件/属性/状态、深度与度量、3D 结构、视角与位姿、机器人中心线索、affordance 等；第二层强调动作理解、局部状态转移、交互后果和物理约束；第三层处理长时序规划、导航、反思、修复和恢复。这个 taxonomy 不是只拿来写 benchmark，而是直接指导 pre-training 与 post-training 的数据管线和 mixture 设计。也就是说，作者试图把“需要什么能力”前置到数据生产与配比，而不是训练完再靠下游补洞。\n\n第二条是模型架构与效率设计。Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 采用 Hy3-A3B 语言骨干和 Hy-ViT2 视觉编码器，并使用 MoE 架构来控制推理成本。文中强调只有 3B activated parameters，但整体能力接近上一代更大的 A32B 方案，说明它更像是在总参数和激活参数之间做了面向部署的折中。这里的关键不是 MoE 本身新，而是把 MoE 放在 embodied VLM 上，目标明确指向低延迟 physical-world agent。与很多只追求离线 benchmark 的 embodied 模型不同，这篇工作从一开始就把 latency-sensitive deployment 作为约束。\n\n从全文给出的信息看，作者还把训练分成 pre-train 和 post-train 两段，并用 taxonomy 去约束数据混合。虽然公开内容里没有把每一类数据规模、采样权重、课程策略完全展开到可复现程度，但可以看出他们不是简单拼接现成 VQA、导航、机器人数据，而是在能力层级上做覆盖和配平。这一点比单纯报一个总 token 数更有信息量。它隐含的判断是：embodied 能力缺口首先是训练信号结构问题，其次才是模型容量问题。","experiments":"实验部分最重要的是评测覆盖和效率对比。作者把评测扩展到 38 个 benchmark，覆盖三层能力 taxonomy。与同规模近期模型 Qwen3.6-A3B、NVIDIA Cosmos 3-8B、Embodied-R1.5-8B 对比时，Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 在 38 个 benchmark 中有 19 个第一、11 个第二，三类能力平均分也最高，平均领先最强对手 Qwen3.6-A3B 4.4%。相对上一代 Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B，平均提升 8.4%。\n\n效率是另一个关键数字。文中强调该模型仅用 3B 激活参数，约为上一代 A32B 的十分之一，但整体性能接近。这说明作者在 embodied 场景下把“能力/时延/激活开销”三者做了比较有效的平衡。对真实机器人或交互系统，这比单纯扩大总参数更有意义。\n\n在更接近 agent 的任务上，作者还评测了视觉语言导航。R2R-CE 上，在 RGB-only 设置下达到当时最优；在 Matterport3D Object Goal Navigation 上表现出较强 zero-shot 能力。这里值得注意的是，导航结果被作者当作 taxonomy 第三层能力的外部验证：如果模型真的学到了动作序列、环境反馈和恢复能力，它应该能迁移到更强交互性的环境中，而不只是做静态图像问答。\n\n不过从预训练研究者角度，也要看到实验的边界。文中给了总体平均提升和 benchmark 排名，但若想判断“到底是 taxonomy 数据设计起主要作用，还是 backbone / MoE / post-train 起主要作用”，还需要更细的消融，例如不同能力层数据去除、pre/post 比例变化、MoE 与 dense 对照、导航能力对静态数据比例的敏感性等。公开内容里这部分信息有限。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一看 Introduction 和 Figure 2，对三层 capability taxonomy 建立清晰心智模型。这是全文真正的主轴。第二看 Data and Taxonomy 部分，重点不是记子任务名，而是看作者如何把能力定义映射到数据构造与 mixture；如果你做多模态预训练，这部分最有参考价值。第三看实验总表和导航实验，关注两件事：一是 38 个 benchmark 的覆盖是否真的对应 taxonomy；二是 3B activated parameters 与上一代大模型的效率对比。\n\n如果阅读时间有限，30 分钟内可以完成一轮泛读：Introduction、taxonomy 图、主实验表、导航结果、结论。若要深挖，建议带着两个问题读：作者的数据 mixture 到底在补什么能力缺口；MoE 在 embodied 场景里是单纯省算力，还是也改变了能力分工。真正值得深挖的点不是某个 benchmark 分数，而是这套“能力定义先行”的训练组织方式能否迁移到更广义的 native multimodal pretrain。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about generic VLM perception or visual QA. It targets a more specific gap: what capabilities a physical-world agent actually needs, and how to train an efficient foundation model around those capabilities. The central issue is that standard VLMs can perceive and answer, but they are weak at action-relevant state understanding, reasoning over environment transitions, and maintaining adaptive behavior over long interaction horizons. That gap limits transfer to navigation, manipulation, and embodied decision-making.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is methodological rather than algorithmic. The paper builds a fairly explicit loop from capability taxonomy to data construction, training mixture, and evaluation. That is useful for anyone working on multimodal pretraining beyond static image-text alignment. Three takeaways matter. First, embodied pretraining cannot be reduced to scaling generic image-text corpora; the data has to be organized around action-relevant state, transitions, and sequential adaptation. Second, an MoE VLM with low activated parameters can still retain strong embodied competence, which matters for latency-constrained deployment. Third, the evaluation suite is aligned with the training taxonomy, making the work a practical template for native multimodal or embodied foundation model development. 的核心。Section 6.1 可快速理解为什么单参数 steering 和 resist-only training 不够；Section 6.3 值得看，因为它暴露了两次推理方法转成单次模型行为时的损失。若关注对齐评测，可补看 Section 6.4 的 SycophancyEval transfer。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies internal incentive compatibility for LLM reports. A model should revise its answer when given legitimate evidence, but should not change merely because the user is confident, prestigious, insistent, or restyles the prompt. The hard part is that resistance and updating are opposing controls: a model can look robust by refusing to update, or look responsive by following pressure.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method, but it is useful for researchers studying how post-training changes the interface between latent beliefs and generated reports. The main value is methodological: it separates belief recovery from report distortion, and validates hidden-state report mediators with causal interventions rather than probe accuracy. That makes it relevant to sycophancy analysis, RLHF side effects, and the design of training signals that should affect reporting behavior without erasing evidence sensitivity.","technical_approach":"The authors build a Bayesian-witness benchmark with a hidden binary state and signals whose likelihood ratios are specified, so the posterior is known by construction. The same user disagreement can be made licensed evidence or forbidden pressure by changing a stated reliability variable. Reports are parsed into answer, confidence, and caveat fields, and a belief-escrow protocol first elicits the model’s own committed report before measuring later distortion. On the representation side, the paper identifies low-rank answer, confidence, and caveat coordinates through interchange interventions, checking sufficiency, exclusion, block-level necessity, and approximate independence. The intervention method is a counterfactual report-coordinate clamp: run the same model on an incentive-neutralized version of the prompt, extract the report coordinate, then clamp the pressured run toward that coordinate. This differs from global steering because the reference is sample-specific and path-specific.","experiments":"Experiments are run on open dense models in the 3B–8B range, including Qwen2.5-3B/7B, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The benchmark uses at least nine matched counterfactual prompt variants, allowing resistance and updating to be scored separately. The main empirical pattern is clear: decoding changes and global steering trade off resistance against evidence responsiveness; resistance-oriented fine-tuning generalizes some robustness but reduces legitimate updating; the counterfactual coordinate clamp is the only tested method that satisfies both controls in the matched setting. The paper also studies compilation of the two-pass procedure into one forward pass and finds it lossy, which points toward training-time internalization as a more durable route. Transfer is checked on SycophancyEval, while larger and non-dense models remain untested.","reading_guide":"Skim time: 30–45 minutes. Read the Bayesian-witness construction in Section 3 first, because it defines what counts as licensed evidence versus forbidden influence. Section 6.1 is the fastest way to see why scalar steering and resistance-only training are insufficient. 的质量，而且最优时长强烈依赖 merge 方法本身。更具体地说，简单平均更偏好欠训练专家；带冲突消解和稀疏化的 TIES / DARE+TIES 反而在明显过拟合之后达到最好结果。论文真正解决的是“单模型最优”和“可合并性最优”并不一致这一问题，并试图给出机制解释。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发主要有两层。第一层是工程层面：如果企业或研究团队在做多域 LoRA 专家训练再 merge，早停点不该再被当成固定 recipe，而应视 merge 算法共同决定的超参。第二层是方法层面：论文把 model merging 从“静态算法比较”推进到“训练轨迹上的 mergeability 分析”，这比单纯比较 TIES、DARE、Soup 的最终分数更有信息量。它说明专家间的多样性、干扰结构和参数符号冲突，会随着训练继续发生系统变化；而 merge 方法的优劣，部分取决于它能否利用这种后期多样性并抑制干扰。量级上，这不是改写预训练范式的工作，但对小中型开源模型的多任务适配、持续增量训练、模块化后训练都有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个模型尺度、5 个任务域、8 个训练时长、5 种 merge 方法，主结果看四个 accuracy 类任务的平均分，Safety Greedy Soup 的峰值更靠近中间。作者强调这种模式在 0.8B、2B、4B 上都能观察到，只是峰值位置和幅度会随尺度变化。Safety 结果也不是例外：拒答率随 merge 方法和训练时长同样呈现方法依赖的最优点，而不是统一地“越早越好”或“越晚越好”。\n\n消融和分析部分的价值在于把现象拆开。一个重要观察是，随着训练超过 T*，单个专家验证性能下降，但专家之间的差异性上升。若 merge 方法没有冲突处理，这种差异性直接变成 destructive interference；若 merge 方法能做稀疏化和符号对齐，差异性的一部分会转成互补性。作者还比较了不同方法在不同时长下的 merged-vs-best-expert 差距，说明“拿最优单专家 checkpoint merge”并不等于“得到最优 merged model”。摘要里没有给出大量具体数值，全文重点也更偏趋势和机制，而不是追求某个单点提升数字。阅读时应把注意力放在曲线形状、峰值位置和分析图，而不是只找一个平均提升百分点。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 最后两段和 Figure 1，先建立一个清晰预期：这篇文章不是在发明新 merge 算法，而是在改写 merge 实验的默认 protocol。然后看
