小时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a more fundamental question than “which merging method works best”: at what training stage should expert models be merged? Standard practice in model merging takes each expert at its own validation-optimal checkpoint and treats that as the natural input to merging. This work shows that, for decoder-only LLM experts, that assumption is unreliable. The best checkpoint for an individual expert is often not the best checkpoint for the merged model, and the optimal training duration depends strongly on the merging algorithm. Simple averaging prefers undertrained experts, while sparsification-based methods such as TIES and DARE+TIES often peak well after the experts have passed their own validation optimum.","importance":"Its main value is methodological rather than benchmark-driven. For practical LLM adaptation pipelines—especially multi-domain LoRA training followed by weight-space merging—the paper argues that early stopping should not be treated as a fixed recipe independent of the merge method. More broadly, it shifts the discussion from static comparisons of merge algorithms to a trajectory-level view of mergeability: how expert diversity, interference, and parameter conflict evolve over training, and how different merging rules exploit or fail under those dynamics. This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to continual adaptation, modular post-training, and the interface between optimization trajectories and parameter-space composition.","technical_approach":"$24","experiments":"$25","reading_guide":"Start with the end of the introduction and the main result figure. un","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-13T21:18:52Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_12166.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是 是否会在正确输入上激活该特征，也没有验证对该特征做干预是否会沿着模型真实计算路径影响输出。论文在一个完全可控的 toy superposition setting 中，把这两件事明确分开，并用因果干预证明：高 cosine match 可以对应因果上几乎无效的 feature，作者把这种现象称为 causal inertness。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于改进 encoder/selection 机制、以及最终输出因果效应之间存在可测的裂缝。这个结论会影响两类工作。第一类是用 SAE 做模型内部表征分析的研究：以后不能只报 cosine recovery。第二类是把 SAE feature 当成可控接口做 steering、editing、safety intervention 的工作：如果 feature 是 read-inert 或 write-inert，那么看起来“找到了”特征，实际却未必能读出或写入模型行为。对基座模型研究者来说，这篇论文的重要性在于它把“解释性 feature 是否真实可操作”从相关性问题推进到因果验证问题。","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读。建议先抓住论文最重要的判断：decoder 的判定标准。这里是全文最关键的创新。\n\n第二步回头看 superposition phase diagram 的复现。重点看高 sparsity convergence artifact 是如何被发现的，训练步数如何随 sparsity 调整，以及 diffuse sharing regime 的几何特征。这个部分会帮助理解为什么某些 feature 即使几何上可恢复，也可能在 encoder 选择机制下失活或竞争失败。\n\n第三步看 TopK-vs-L1 复现和 activation refinement 测试。这里要注意作者不是简单说 TopK 更好，而是在问：哪一种 SAE 的 decoder recovery 更接近真实可干预 feature。若你平时只看 reconstruction / sparsity / cosine，这一节会补上 encoder 行为这一块。\n\n第四步看 taxonomy 和工具化部分。structural inertness vs competitive inertness、read-inertness vs write-inertness 这四个概念值得记住，因为它们把“为什么干预无效”拆成了可诊断的问题。最后再看真实模型上的 gpt2-small-res-jb 审计和 atom collision 现象，判断 toy 结论在真实模型里是否已有早期迹象。\n\n如果时间有限，优先读 Sections 6、7、10、11，再补 4 和 5。若要复现实验，Section 8 的 reproducibility 约定也值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central evaluation mistake in sparse autoencoder research: a decoder atom being geometrically aligned with a ground-truth feature direction does not imply that the SAE actually recovers the feature as used by the model. Current practice relies heavily on correlational recovery metrics such as unsigned cosine similarity. Those metrics validate decoder geometry, but they do not validate encoder activation behavior, selection dynamics, or whether intervening on the recovered feature changes the model’s actual output. In a fully controlled superposition toy setting, the paper cleanly separates these claims and shows by intervention that highly matched features can still be causally inert.","importance":"Its main contribution is methodological calibration for mechanistic interpretability rather than a new pretraining recipe. A large fraction of current SAE-based interpretability work implicitly treats correlational recovery as sufficient evidence of feature discovery. This paper shows that the inference is too strong. Even in the easiest possible setting, where ground truth is known, decoder alignment and causal efficacy can diverge by a measurable margin. That matters for two lines of work. First, studies that use SAEs to characterize internal representations can no longer stop at cosine recovery. Second, work that treats SAE features as control handles for steering, editing, or safety interventions needs to distinguish features that are merely geometrically plausible from features that are actually readable or writable in the model’s computation. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-14T07:24:32Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_12447.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文关心的不是“LLM 的置信度能不能校准”，而是更底层的问题：模型输出中的 confidence signal 到底在表示什么。作者把候选信号聚焦到二分类答案的 answer-logit difference，记作 LD = lA - lB，问它究竟只是一个启发式偏好分数，还是一个潜在决策变量的单调读出；如果是后者，那么它在原则上足以支持规范意义上的 posterior confidence 计算。论文借用计算神经科学里的 statistical decision confidence, SDC 框架，在若干可明确定义“证据强度”的感知判别任务和记忆决策任务上检验这一点。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响有限，它不是训练方法论文，也不提供新的优化 recipe。但它对理解 logits、uncertainty 和 abstention 的计算含义有价值，尤其适合关心 calibration、verifier、self-evaluation、test-time decision policy 的研究者。更具体地说，这篇工作试图把“logit gap 能预测正确率”提升为“logit gap 是否对应某种可解释的内部决策变量”。如果这个命题在一定任务族上成立，那么很多基于 logits 的拒答、路由、采样控制、风险敏感解码就不只是经验技巧，而有更明确的计算解释。反过来，作者也清楚展示了这个框架的边界：在复杂视觉推理里，LD 仍然预测正确性，但不再满足 SDC 的完整几何签名。这一点很重要，因为它提醒人们不要把简单任务上的 confidence 结论直接外推到开放式推理。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者没有发明新的 confidence estimator，而是把现有最基础的信号——答案 token 的 logit 差——放到一个规范框架里做判别。SDC 的核心思想是：系统内部存在一个带噪的 latent decision variable，它是对任务相关证据的内部表征；在给定这个变量后，当前选择为真的后验概率就被唯一确定。因此，如果某个可观测信号是这个潜变量的单调读出，那么它不仅经验上能预测正确与否，而且在信息论意义上携带了计算 posterior confidence 所需的充分信息。\n\n作者据此提出四类由弱到强的定性签名来检验 LD 是否像潜在决策变量。可见文本里明确提到 psychometric function，以及最关键的 correct/error folded-X pattern。folded-X 是 SDC 文献里的诊断性现象：随着刺激证据增强，正确试次上的 confidence 上升，而错误试次上的 confidence 反而下降，二者形成折叠的 X 形关系。如果 LD 只是某种粗糙偏好分数，这种结构未必会稳定出现。作者在三个感知判别任务和一个记忆型决策任务上，对三种多模态非推理模型和一种多模态推理模型做测试。任务设计的关键是实验者能显式控制 stimulus strength，也就是支持 A 或 B 的证据强度，从而给出一个明确的 normative process model。这样就能把模型的选择概率、LD 大小、正确率和刺激强度放在同一坐标系里检验。\n\n论文还加入了行为层面的 abstention 实验。模型被允许在低置信时放弃作答，作者观察它是否会利用低 LD 的样本进行近似最优的拒答，从而显著提高 committed responses 的准确率。这个设计很关键，因为它把“LD 是否可解释”从静态相关性推进到决策后果：如果 LD 真是有意义的 confidence readout，它应该能支持合理的 selective prediction 行为。","experiments":"主实验覆盖四类任务：三个感知判别任务加一个记忆决策任务，模型包括三种多模态非推理模型和一种多模态推理模型。作者报告，在这些结构较简单、证据轴清晰的任务上，LD 满足 SDC 预测的关键签名，包括最有诊断性的 correct/error folded-X pattern。这意味着在这些设置里，answer logits 更像是潜在决策变量的单调读出，而不只是“模型更偏好哪个答案”的启发式分数。\n\n另一个重要结果是 abstention。允许模型拒答后，它会把低 LD 的样本优先筛掉，留下的 committed responses 准确率接近天花板。这里的意义不在于“拒答能提准”，而在于这种拒答行为接近规范意义上的最优选择，说明 LD 对行为策略确实有用。\n\n边界条件同样值得注意。在复杂视觉推理任务中，LD 依然能在控制客观任务难度后预测正确性，说明它不是无效信号；但 SDC 的完整几何签名不再成立。作者据此指出：当任务缺乏清晰的 normative process model，或者推理链条更长、潜变量不再是一维证据积累时，把 LD 解释为潜在决策变量的单调读出就会变得不充分。摘要和可见正文没有给出大量具体数值，更强调定性模式、统计显著性和行为趋势。阅读时应重点看图形关系和条件对照，而不是只盯 calibration 指标。","reading_guide":"这篇文章适合带着一个明确问题去读：logit gap 到底是 confidence，还是只是 preference。先看 Introduction 前半和 Figure 1，弄清 SDC 框架中的 latent decision variable、posterior confidence、stimulus strength 三者关系。然后重点看作者定义的四个 signature，尤其是 folded-X pattern；这是全文最关键的判别标准。\n\n实验部分建议按任务难度读。先看感知判别任务，因为这些任务的规范模型最清楚，最能说明 LD 是否像决策变量。再看记忆任务，理解这种结论是否能超出纯感知场景。最后看复杂视觉推理部分，把它当作边界测试，而不是主结论。若只想抓住论文主旨，读主文图表和方法定义大约 30 分钟；若要真正判断这个框架能否迁移到 LLM 自评、拒答和 verifier 设计，还需要细读方法细节、统计检验和 abstention 设置，大约 1 小时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about whether LLM confidence is calibrated. It asks a deeper question: what does the confidence signal encoded in model outputs actually represent? The authors focus on the answer-logit difference, LD = lA - lB, in binary decisions, and ask whether it is merely a heuristic preference score or a monotonic readout of a latent decision variable. If it is the latter, then it is, in principle, sufficient for computing normative posterior confidence. To test this, the paper imports the statistical decision confidence (SDC) framework from computational neuroscience and evaluates LD on tasks where stimulus evidence can be explicitly controlled.","importance":"Its relevance to core pretraining is indirect, but it matters for anyone working on uncertainty, abstention, self-evaluation, verifier design, or risk-sensitive inference. The paper tries to move beyond the usual empirical claim that logit gaps correlate with correctness. Instead, it asks whether logit gaps have a stronger computational interpretation. If that interpretation holds in some task families, then logit-based abstention, routing, and confidence-aware decoding are not just heuristics—they are grounded in a more principled account of what the model is representing. Equally important, the paper is careful about the boundary: in complex visual reasoning, LD still predicts correctness, but the full SDC signatures break down. That makes the work useful as a map of where simple confidence interpretations hold and where they stop being adequate.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"$2c","reading_guide":"Read this paper with one concrete question in mind: is logit gap confidence, or just preference? Start with the introduction and the first conceptual figure to understand the relationship among stimulus strength, latent decision variable, and posterior confidence under SDC. Then focus on the four signatures the authors test, especially the folded-X pattern. That is the central diagnostic tool in the paper.\n\nFor the experiments, read in order of task simplicity. The perceptual discrimination tasks are the cleanest because the normative model is explicit. Then move to the memory task to see whether the interpretation extends beyond pure perception. Treat the complex visual reasoning section as a boundary test rather than the main positive result. A focused skim of the main text and figures should take about 30 minutes. ψ，先采样多条路径，再用 verifier 判断哪些路径 viable，最后对成功路径按 mode map 分组。指标分成两部分：一是 verified reachability，即到达可行 continuation 的概率；二是 verified-mode diversity，即成功路径在不同语义模式上的熵。这样的分解把“能不能成功”和“成功方式是否多样”分开。论文还用 Mirror Theory 给出一套形式化语境：mirror 被定义为 continuation law，而不是静态 hidden vector；intuition 表示证据给出的局部约束，taste 表示在可行 continuation 中选择哪些不变量需要保留，reflection 是在这些约束和选择压力下反复更新。对 LLM 研究更实用的是 VPE 的操作化部分，而不是哲学化的术语框架。","experiments":"实验使用 Qwen2.5-Instruct 系列在 GSM8K 上做采样。设置为 30 道题，每题 32 条 rollout，temperature 0.8，top-p 0.95，并用最终数值正确性作为 verifier，用启发式 mode map 给正确解分组。比较两个最大生成长度：96 tokens 和 160 tokens。96-token 设置包含 Qwen2.5-0.5B 和 1.5B；160-token 设置包含 0.5B、1.5B 和 3B。主要现象是：从 96 增加到 160 tokens 后，0.5B 和 1.5B 的 verified probability、pass@32、非零成功覆盖、verified-mode entropy 和 smoothed VPE 都提升。160-token 设置下，1.5B 在核心组成项上优于被测模型；3B 并不占优，它的平均 verified probability 高于 0.5B，但题目覆盖和模式熵低于 1.5B，整体 VPE 也低于 1.5B。文中给出的片段没有包含完整表格数值，因此不宜外推到模型规模规律。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15 到 20 分钟。优先看 VPE 定义、verifier 和 mode map 的设定，以及 GSM8K 实验部分。若只关心 LLM 评测，可以跳过大部分 Mirror Theory 的抽象术语，把它当作一种“有限预算下的验证成功率加成功模式熵”指标来看。需要重点检查两个薄弱点：mode map 是否稳定，verifier 是否只验证答案而没有验证推理过程。若未来用于预训练或 post-train 反馈，最值得深挖的是 VPE 是否能区分 reward hacking 式的单一路径收敛和真实的解空间扩展。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper proposes a way to measure how many verified continuation paths a model can sustain under a finite rollout budget. Instead of focusing only on loss, accuracy, or pass@k, it asks how likely the model is to reach a viable continuation and how diverse the verified successful modes are.","importance":"The direct impact on pretraining is limited because the work does not introduce a new objective, data recipe, tokenizer, or architecture. Its main relevance is as an evaluation and post-training diagnostic. It separates accessible verified capability from parameter count and shows that the measured capability depends on the sampling protocol, token budget, verifier, and semantic mode map. This could be useful for reasoning-model evaluation, especially when comparing whether training improves only the probability of one successful path or also broadens the set of verified solution modes.","technical_approach":"The proposed metric is viable path entropy, or VPE. Given a finite rollout budget B, horizon T, continuation law, verifier V, and mode map ψ, the procedure samples multiple continuations, filters them through the verifier, and groups the successful continuations into semantic modes. The metric has two components: verified reachability, which measures the probability of reaching a viable continuation, and verified-mode diversity, which measures the entropy of modes among successful rollouts. The surrounding Mirror Theory framework treats a mirror as a continuation law rather than a static representation. In that framework, intuition supplies local underdetermining constraints from evidence, taste selects which invariants should survive, and reflection repeatedly resolves the admissible continuations. For practical LLM work, the operational VPE definition is more important than the terminology around the theory.","experiments":"The empirical study samples Qwen2.5-Instruct models on GSM8K. It uses 30 problems, 32 rollouts per problem, temperature 0.8, top-p 0.95, final numeric correctness as the verifier, and a heuristic mode map for grouping verified solutions. Two generation horizons are compared: 96 and 160 maximum new tokens. The 96-token setting includes Qwen2.5-0.5B and Qwen2.5-1.5B. The 160-token setting includes 0.5B, 1.5B, and 3B. Increasing the horizon from 96 to 160 improves verified probability, pass@32, nonzero verified coverage, verified-mode entropy, and smoothed VPE for both 0.5B and 1.5B. At 160 tokens, the 1.5B model is strongest on the reported core components. The 3B model is not the best under this protocol: it has higher average verified probability than 0.5B but lower breadth across problems and lower verified-mode entropy than 1.5B. The provided text does not include full numeric tables, so the result should not be read as a general scaling claim.","reading_guide":"A 15 to 20 minute skim is enough for most LLM researchers. Focus on the VPE definition, the verifier and mode-map construction, and the GSM8K experiment. If the goal is practical evaluation, much of the Mirror Theory scaffolding can be skipped. The two details to inspect are whether the heuristic mode map is stable and whether the verifier checks only the final answer or also the reasoning process. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. feature family 上；第二，是否必须依赖标签监督，还是只靠任务不变性就够；第三，这种先验必须在训练的哪个阶段介入，是否存在 critical window；第四，这个解释在不同任务、架构和归一化设置下是否仍然成立。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于 modular arithmetic curriculum 是否比后期更关键。量级上，这不是直接可迁移到 LLM recipe 的工程结论，但它提供了一个较扎实的机制模板，用来理解为什么某些 auxiliary objective、representation regularizer 或 invariance signal Building on the companion paper, the authors treat grokking as a delayed formation of task-structured representations, then test four specific conditions behind that claim: whether a prior must match the task’s feature family rather than merely impose coherent structure; whether labels are necessary or a label-free invariance signal is enough; whether the prior must act early in training; and whether the account survives changes in task, architecture, and normalization.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is conceptual rather than directly recipe-level.
